Title: Prima speditione all'Indie Orientali del P. F. Giuseppe di Santa Maria, Carmelitano Scalzo, Delegato Apostolico ne' Regni de' Malavari, ordinata da Nostro Signore Alessandro Settimo
Creator: Giuseppe di Santa Maria Sebastiani, O.C.D. (1623-1689), Discalced Carmelite, apostolic delegate to the St. Thomas Christians of Malabar 1656-1658 and afterwards Bishop of Hierapolis and vicar apostolic of Malabar (first-person account)
Publisher: In Roma: Nella Stamperia di Filippo Maria Mancini, con licenza de' Superiori, a spese de' Signori Giuseppe e Sebastiano Sebastiani da Caprarola, nipoti dell'autore
Publication Date: 1666 (title page 'M. DC LXVI')
Rights: Public Domain (published 1666, more than 95 years before 2026; public domain by date)
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_8zYE6AfFRikC, file bub_gb_8zYE6AfFRikC_djvu.txt (Google scan of the National Central Library of Rome copy, bound as the first part of 'Speditioni all'Indie Orientali di Monsignor Sebastiani'), fetched directly by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session), 2026-10-16; host rights statement: licenseurl http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/ (Public Domain Mark 1.0); no possible-copyright-status field.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged apart from this header (no long-s characters present; the OCR renders the seventeenth-century long s as 'f' throughout and that pattern is left as found; nothing retyped, dropped or corrected). Rights basis restated in one checkable sentence: the title page inside this file reads 'IN ROMA, Nella Stamperia di Filippo Maria Mancini. M. DC LXVI', so the work is public domain by date.
Content note: Italian throughout. Three books, with a 'Tavola de' capitoli' at the end: Libro Primo, the foundation and the several states of the Christianity of the Serra (the St. Thomas Christians), its fall (the revolt of 1653 and the Archdeacon's elevation), how the news reached Rome, and the author's journey out by Malta, Syria, Aleppo, Nineveh, Baghdad, Basra, Persia and Surat to Cananor and the Serra; Libro Secondo, the mission of 1657-1658 (the attempt to reduce 'l'Intruso', the Archdeacon Thomas; the assemblies of the churches at Mattancherry, Mangate and other places; the author's stay in the fort of Cochin; a description of the Serra and of Malabar customs; his decision to return to Rome leaving a substitute); Libro Terzo, the return journey by Cananor, Goa, Surat, Muscat, Persia and Basra. A Carmelite eyewitness writing for Rome against the Archdeacon's party; primary for the mission side of 1656-1658 and for the earliest printed Catholic account of the 1653 revolt, hostile to the community's own choice. OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 percent and found readable but rough (share of word-like tokens 0.79, broken words and misread letters throughout); a second scan of the same edition (bub_gb_Pygo81gr7tUC) sampled slightly worse. Second witness for exact wording until a passage is checked against the page images. No public-domain English translation is known to exist (archive.org searched 2026-10-16: none found).
